Native orbit smoothing comparison

Predeclared primary cell: leo500 / disturbed / blackout. Other cells are secondary.

Frozen protocol: unavailable

Engineering validation before calibration. No production comparison has been run and no accuracy-improvement claim is supported.

Calibration and frozen evaluation have not started; no comparative scientific result is available.

Both primary methods pass fresh normal/masked one-hour independent synthetic fits. Four native scheduling replays give exactly equal final estimates and covariance when uncertainty is deferred to final confirmation.

The full-day blackout density engineering fit failed before shared nominal closure and then oscillated after closure. Adaptive numerical damping is undergoing validation; these failures remain recorded and do not count as scientific results.

All sixteen current-binary full-day synthetic force/interpolation refinements pass. Current full-flight force, thermal, state-grid and combined refinements pass with an explicit0.1-second thermal maximum step.

Current-runtime fully nonlinear finite differences for one development density knot show about0.027percent relative discrepancy from the shared low-degree gravity-gradient transition approximation. This is not an exact-derivative or global covariance-calibration claim.

An April24 pulse engineering fit exercises independent correctedSLR with correlated predictive covariance and trueCRD session summaries; GNV agreement is reported separately. May spacecraft measurements and references remain unopened.

One April24 developmentGNV orbit was inspected earlier for an initialization diagnostic; its post-fit engineering use is also disclosed. Neither use enters an estimator or calibration score.

Evaluation SLR correction sources are now metadata-preflighted, hashed before production fits and verified offline during evaluation; reference orbits and SLR numerical observations remain unavailable to fitting.

The environment restart cleared temporary local runtimes. Persistent inputs and evidence were preserved, missing synthetic/refinement experiments were regenerated, and prior interrupted attempts remain identified.

The comparison is incomplete. No final comparative claim is supported.

Numerical acceptance gates are missing or have not all passed.

Method superiority is not inferred automatically from completion or a surviving subset of fits.

Paired gap position errors

Differences are method A minus B. Intervals resample independent paired seeds, with no time samples treated as separate trials.

Blackout metrics are not applicable to normal-coverage cases. Missing planned seeds are counted even when both methods have no result.

No records available.

Position uncertainty at fixed epochs

95% position ellipsoid coverage, with Wilson intervals across independent seeds at each fixed epoch. Failed or unavailable seeds remain listed.

No records available.

Whole-arc and blackout accuracy and uncertainty

Each row is one independent seed. RTN means radial, along-track and cross-track. NLL and NEES are averages of marginal position scores across the indicated arc, not independent time-sample trials. Interval widths and ellipsoids are conditional on selected hyperparameters; model-selection uncertainty is excluded.

No records available.

Paired confidence intervals for all metrics

Units follow the named metric. A smaller NEES or a narrower interval does not by itself imply better uncertainty calibration.

No records available.

Runtime includes native outer iterations. Peak memory is the largest sampled native-process resident set; it excludes the Python wrapper and shared filesystem caches.
